Clear stars

A star needs five prior games, an 80% clear rate, the last five median over the number, a cushion under 3.5, and a number that has not risen by a point. Blocks are not starred. A U needs the minutes short on more than one recent game, or a rebound or steal number at least a point above the recent average. One short night does not mark points, PRA, threes, or assists. An arrow is a minute ramp, which is a lean, not a star.
